<laravel-boost-guidelines>
=== foundation rules ===

# Laravel Boost Guidelines

## Foundational Context

This application is a Laravel application running on PHP 8.5. Always use the APIs that match the installed major version of each package — do not assume a version.

Before relying on a package's API, confirm its installed version:
- PHP packages: run `vendor/bin/sail composer show --direct` to list direct dependencies with versions, or `vendor/bin/sail composer show <vendor/package>` for a single package.
- JS packages: check `package.json` for the installed versions.

## Skills Activation

This project has domain-specific skills available in `**/skills/**`. You MUST activate the relevant skill whenever you work in that domain—don't wait until you're stuck.

## Conventions

- You must follow all existing code conventions used in this application. When creating or editing a file, check sibling files for the correct structure, approach, and naming.
- Use descriptive names for variables and methods. For example, `isRegisteredForDiscounts`, not `discount()`.
- Check for existing components to reuse before writing a new one.

## Verification Scripts

- Do not create verification scripts or tinker when tests cover that functionality and prove they work. Unit and feature tests are more important.

## Application Structure & Architecture

- Stick to existing directory structure; don't create new base folders without approval.
- Do not change the application's dependencies without approval.

## Frontend Bundling

- If a frontend change doesn't show in the UI or you get a "Unable to locate file in Vite manifest" error, run `vendor/bin/sail npm run build` or ask the user to run `vendor/bin/sail npm run dev` or `vendor/bin/sail composer run dev`.

## Documentation Files

- You must only create documentation files if explicitly requested by the user.

=== boost rules ===

# Laravel Boost

## Tools

- Laravel Boost is an MCP server with tools designed specifically for this application. Prefer Boost tools over manual alternatives like shell commands or file reads.
- Use `database-query` to run read-only queries against the database instead of writing raw SQL in tinker.
- Use `database-schema` to inspect table structure before writing migrations or models.
- Use `get-absolute-url` to resolve the correct scheme, domain, and port for project URLs. Always use this before sharing a URL with the user.
- Use `browser-logs` to read browser logs, errors, and exceptions. Only recent logs are useful, ignore old entries.

## Searching Documentation (IMPORTANT)

- Use `search-docs` before changes that depend on Laravel ecosystem APIs, behavior, configuration, or version-specific syntax. Skip it for copy-only edits and other changes where package documentation is irrelevant. Reuse sufficient results already in context instead of searching again.
- Pass a `packages` array to scope results when you know which packages are relevant.
- Use multiple broad, topic-based queries: `['rate limiting', 'routing rate limiting', 'routing']`. Expect the most relevant results first.
- Do not add package names to queries because package info is already shared. Use `test resource table`, not `filament 4 test resource table`.

### Search Syntax

1. Use words for auto-stemmed AND logic: `rate limit` matches both "rate" AND "limit".
2. Use `"quoted phrases"` for exact position matching: `"infinite scroll"` requires adjacent words in order.
3. Combine words and phrases for mixed queries: `middleware "rate limit"`.
4. Use multiple queries for OR logic: `queries=["authentication", "middleware"]`.

## Project Rules

- This project contains committed, area-grouped rules in `.ai/rules` when that directory exists, including path-scoped framework guidelines under `.ai/rules/boost`. Before you enter plan mode or create/edit any file, you MUST first: open @.ai/rules/index.md (it maps file globs to rule files), read every rule file whose globs cover the path(s) in scope, and run `grep -rin 'keyword' .ai/rules` to catch what a path match alone misses. Do not write code until you have read and are following every matching rule. If `.ai/rules` does not exist, continue without it.

## Artisan

- Run Artisan commands directly via the command line (e.g., `vendor/bin/sail artisan route:list`). Use `vendor/bin/sail artisan list` to discover available commands and `vendor/bin/sail artisan [command] --help` to check parameters.
- Inspect routes with `vendor/bin/sail artisan route:list`. Filter with: `--method=GET`, `--name=users`, `--path=api`, `--except-vendor`, `--only-vendor`.
- Read configuration values using dot notation: `vendor/bin/sail artisan config:show app.name`, `vendor/bin/sail artisan config:show database.default`. Or read config files directly from the `config/` directory.

## Tinker

- Execute PHP in app context for debugging and testing code. Do not create models without user approval, prefer tests with factories instead. Prefer existing Artisan commands over custom tinker code.
- Always use single quotes to prevent shell expansion: `vendor/bin/sail artisan tinker --execute 'Your::code();'`
  - Double quotes for PHP strings inside: `vendor/bin/sail artisan tinker --execute 'User::where("active", true)->count();'`

=== php rules ===

# PHP

- Always use curly braces for control structures, even for single-line bodies.
- Use PHP 8 constructor property promotion: `public function __construct(public GitHub $github) { }`. Do not leave empty zero-parameter `__construct()` methods unless the constructor is private.
- Use explicit return type declarations and type hints for all method parameters: `function isAccessible(User $user, ?string $path = null): bool`
- Use TitleCase for Enum keys: `FavoritePerson`, `BestLake`, `Monthly`.
- Prefer PHPDoc blocks over inline comments. Only add inline comments for exceptionally complex logic.
- Use array shape type definitions in PHPDoc blocks.

=== deployments rules ===

# Deployment

- Laravel can be deployed using [Laravel Cloud](https://cloud.laravel.com/), which is the fastest way to deploy and scale production Laravel applications.
- Activate the `deploying-to-cloud` skill whenever deploying to Laravel Cloud, configuring Cloud environments or resources, using the Cloud CLI, or troubleshooting Cloud deployments.

=== sail rules ===

# Laravel Sail

- This project runs inside Laravel Sail's Docker containers. You MUST execute all commands through Sail.
- Start services using `vendor/bin/sail up -d` and stop them with `vendor/bin/sail stop`.
- Open the application in the browser by running `vendor/bin/sail open`.
- Always prefix PHP, Artisan, Composer, and Node commands with `vendor/bin/sail`. Examples:
    - Run Artisan Commands: `vendor/bin/sail artisan migrate`
    - Install Composer packages: `vendor/bin/sail composer install`
    - Execute Node commands: `vendor/bin/sail npm run dev`
    - Execute PHP scripts: `vendor/bin/sail php [script]`
- View all available Sail commands by running `vendor/bin/sail` without arguments.

=== tests rules ===

# Test Enforcement

- Add or update tests for behavior and logic changes when a test provides meaningful regression coverage.
- Pure copy, styling, and layout-only changes do not require new or updated tests.
- When test coverage applies, run the affected tests and ensure they pass.
- Test the changed behavior and its important failure modes, but do not add tests beyond them.
- Read the `testing-best-practices` skill before writing tests.

=== laravel/core rules ===

# Do Things the Laravel Way

- Use `vendor/bin/sail artisan make:` commands to create new files (i.e. migrations, controllers, models, etc.). You can list available Artisan commands using `vendor/bin/sail artisan list` and check their parameters with `vendor/bin/sail artisan [command] --help`.
- If you're creating a generic PHP class, use `vendor/bin/sail artisan make:class`.
- Pass `--no-interaction` to all Artisan commands to ensure they work without user input. You should also pass the correct `--options` to ensure correct behavior.

### Model Creation

- When creating new models, create useful factories and seeders for them too. Ask the user if they need any other things, using `vendor/bin/sail artisan make:model --help` to check the available options.

## APIs & Eloquent Resources

- For APIs, default to using Eloquent API Resources and API versioning unless existing API routes do not, then you should follow existing application convention.

## URL Generation

- When generating links to other pages, prefer named routes and the `route()` function.

## Testing

- When creating models for tests, use the factories for the models. Check if the factory has custom states that can be used before manually setting up the model.
- Faker: Use methods such as `$this->faker->word()` or `fake()->randomDigit()`. Follow existing conventions whether to use `$this->faker` or `fake()`.
- When creating tests, make use of `vendor/bin/sail artisan make:test [options] {name}` to create a feature test, and pass `--unit` to create a unit test. Most tests should be feature tests.

=== pint/core rules ===

# Laravel Pint Code Formatter

- If you have modified any PHP files, you must run `vendor/bin/sail bin pint --dirty --format agent` before finalizing changes to ensure your code matches the project's expected style.
- Do not run `vendor/bin/sail bin pint --test --format agent`, simply run `vendor/bin/sail bin pint --format agent` to fix any formatting issues.

=== phpunit/core rules ===

# PHPUnit

- This project uses PHPUnit. Create tests with `vendor/bin/sail artisan make:test --phpunit {name}`.
- Do not include the test suite directory in `{name}`. Use `SomeFeatureTest`, not `Feature/SomeFeatureTest`.
- Read the `testing-best-practices` skill for guidance on coverage, naming, structure, dependency isolation, and review.

## Running Tests

- Run the narrowest set of tests that covers the change. Pass a file path or `--filter=testName` to `vendor/bin/sail artisan test --compact`.
- Rerun a test after each change to it.
- Run `vendor/bin/sail bin phpunit` to call the test runner directly. It accepts the same file path and `--filter=testName` arguments.

</laravel-boost-guidelines>

# Build browser assets in a separate Node stage so Node is not part of the PHP runtime image.
FROM node:22-alpine AS frontend

WORKDIR /app

COPY package.json vite.config.js ./
COPY resources ./resources
COPY public ./public

RUN npm install --ignore-scripts \
    && npm run build

# The final image runs the Laravel app under Apache with PHP 8.5.
FROM php:8.5-apache

RUN apt-get update \
    && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
        libcurl4-openssl-dev \
        libicu-dev \
        libonig-dev \
        libpq-dev \
        libzip-dev \
    && docker-php-ext-install -j"$(nproc)" \
        bcmath \
        curl \
        intl \
        mbstring \
        pdo_pgsql \
        zip \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

COPY --from=composer:2 /usr/bin/composer /usr/bin/composer

WORKDIR /var/www/html

COPY composer.json composer.lock ./
COPY . .

RUN composer install \
    --no-dev \
    --no-interaction \
    --prefer-dist \
    --optimize-autoloader

COPY --from=frontend /app/public/build ./public/build

# Render sends web traffic to port 10000; point Apache at Laravel's public document root.
RUN sed -ri 's/Listen 80/Listen 10000/' /etc/apache2/ports.conf \
    && sed -ri 's/<VirtualHost \*:80>/<VirtualHost *:10000>/' /etc/apache2/sites-available/000-default.conf \
    && sed -ri 's!/var/www/html!/var/www/html/public!g' /etc/apache2/sites-available/000-default.conf \
    && sed -ri 's/AllowOverride None/AllowOverride All/' /etc/apache2/apache2.conf \
    && a2enmod rewrite \
    && mkdir -p storage/framework/cache storage/framework/sessions storage/framework/views storage/logs bootstrap/cache \
    && chown -R www-data:www-data storage bootstrap/cache

EXPOSE 10000

# Apply pending schema changes before accepting requests, then keep Apache in the foreground.
CMD ["sh", "-c", "php artisan migrate --force && exec apache2-foreground"]

FILE: README.md
SIZE: 7100 bytes
==============================================================================
# GuidanceSched

GuidanceSched is an academic prototype for a school guidance center. It demonstrates consultation scheduling, account management, confidential case metadata, follow-up records, role-based dashboards, and a small JSON API. It is not a production counseling platform and has not completed an institutional privacy review.

## Roles

The application uses three role values: `admin`, `teacher`, and `student`. The administrator role includes guidance personnel functions and account administration. Teachers and students can see and manage only their own consultation requests. Only administrators can access case descriptions and follow-up notes. There is no public registration; accounts are created by an administrator.

## Requirements and local setup

The checked-in Sail configuration uses PHP 8.5 and PostgreSQL 18. The locked Composer dependencies require PHP 8.4.1 or newer, and `composer.json` declares `^8.4.1`. Use Sail or another PHP 8.4.1+ runtime; PHP 8.1 cannot run Artisan with this lock file.

1. Copy `.env.example` to `.env` if needed. It is configured for the private Compose PostgreSQL service; change the development database password if desired.
2. Install Composer dependencies before starting Sail. This provides the Sail runtime files used by Compose:

   ```sh
   composer install
   ```

3. Start Sail and finish setup:

   ```sh
   ./vendor/bin/sail up -d --build
   ./vendor/bin/sail artisan key:generate
   ./vendor/bin/sail artisan migrate --seed
   ./vendor/bin/sail npm install
   ./vendor/bin/sail npm run build
   ```

4. Open the configured `APP_URL` (Sail defaults to `http://localhost`).

The PostgreSQL port in `compose.yaml` binds to `127.0.0.1` for local development only; the database container is also on the private Compose network. Do not publish port 5432 on a public interface in a deployment.

### Synthetic demonstration accounts

`./vendor/bin/sail artisan db:seed` creates these accounts only if the email address does not already exist:

| Role | Email | Password |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Administrator / guidance personnel | `admin@guidancesched.test` | `ChangeMe123!` |
| Teacher | `teacher@guidancesched.test` | `ChangeMe123!` |
| Student | `student@guidancesched.test` | `ChangeMe123!` |

These are fictional demo credentials. Change them before exposing a development instance to anyone else. The seeder adds only synthetic consultation, case, and follow-up records.

## Appointment workflow

Appointment statuses are `pending`, `approved`, `completed`, and `cancelled`. New requests start as `pending`. Administrators can approve pending requests, complete approved requests, or cancel pending/approved requests. Teachers and students may edit or cancel only their own pending requests. Completed and cancelled requests are terminal.

## REST API

All API routes are under `/api` and use HTTP Basic authentication against existing user accounts. Send `Accept: application/json`; use HTTPS whenever credentials cross a network. The API enforces the same ownership rules as the browser interface. Confidential case and follow-up endpoints require the `admin` role. Appointment JSON deliberately omits the internal `notes` field.

| Method and path | Purpose and request fields | Authorization |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `GET /api/appointments` | List; optional `status` query filter for staff | Students see only assigned date/time and status; teachers/admins see all requests |
| `POST /api/appointments` | Create for a student; required `student_id` and `title`; optional `reason`. Teachers must include `scheduled_at`; administrators may leave it unassigned. | Teacher/admin |
| `GET /api/appointments/{id}` | Student response contains only `scheduled_at` and `status`; staff can view request details | Assigned owner, teacher, or admin |
| `PATCH /api/appointments/{id}` | Teacher: `scheduled_at`; admin: `status`, `scheduled_at`, `assigned_to`, `notes` | Teacher or admin |
| `PATCH /api/appointments/{id}/cancel` | Cancel a request; no body required | Admin only |
| `GET /api/cases` | Search/filter with optional `status`, `search` | Admin |
| `GET /api/cases/{id}` | View case details and follow-up history | Admin |
| `PATCH /api/cases/{id}/status` | Required `status`: `open`, `in_progress`, or `closed` | Admin |
| `GET /api/cases/{id}/follow-ups` | List dated follow-ups | Admin |
| `POST /api/cases/{id}/follow-ups` | Required `follow_up_date`, `notes`; optional `action_taken` | Admin |

JSON responses use a `data` key for individual resources and Laravel's standard pagination object for lists. Example appointment response:

```json
{
  "data": {
    "id": 17,
    "student_id": 4,
    "title": "Study planning consultation",
    "reason": "Discuss study routines",
    "scheduled_at": "2026-10-25T09:00:00.000000Z",
    "duration_minutes": 30,
    "status": "pending",
    "created_at": "2026-10-23T08:00:00.000000Z"
  }
}
```

Validation errors return HTTP 422, unauthenticated requests are challenged with HTTP Basic authentication, and unauthorized role/ownership attempts return HTTP 403.

## Tests

Run the automated suite in the supported PHP/Sail environment:

```sh
./vendor/bin/sail artisan test
```

The test environment should use a separate test database, never the development database. Check the test result output before reporting acceptance criteria as passed.

## Basic load check

For a repeatable local smoke test, use only the seeded synthetic admin account and synthetic database. With ApacheBench (`ab`) installed and the app running locally, issue a small authenticated API workload:

```sh
ab -n 100 -c 5 -A 'admin@guidancesched.test:ChangeMe123!' -H 'Accept: application/json' http://127.0.0.1/api/appointments
```

Record the date, runtime/PHP version, database, concurrency, request count, failed requests, requests per second, and latency summary. This repository has not recorded a load-test result yet; the current shell lacks compatible PHP and Docker, so the procedure has not been run here. Do not run a load test against real student data or a shared deployment without approval.

## Backup and restore

Back up PostgreSQL with `pg_dump` to a protected location and restrict access to the dump because it contains personal and case information. Test restoration only into a separate empty test database, verify the restored data, and document the date and outcome. Never restore over the active project database without an explicit backup and approval.

Example local dump and safe test restore (replace names with values from the local environment):

```sh
docker compose exec -T pgsql pg_dump -U DB_USER DB_NAME > guidance-backup.sql
docker compose exec -T pgsql psql -U DB_USER -d GUIDANCE_TEST_DB < guidance-backup.sql
```

## Privacy and deployment limits

Use synthetic records in demos. Keep the database on a private network and serve the application through HTTPS in any shared environment. Review account access, logging, retention, backups, and school policy before using real data. This prototype does not claim production readiness or formal compliance certification.

# Local development stack: the Laravel container and PostgreSQL share a private Compose network.
services:
    laravel.test:
        build:
            context: './vendor/laravel/sail/runtimes/8.5'
            dockerfile: Dockerfile
            args:
                WWWGROUP: '${WWWGROUP}'
        image: 'sail-8.5/app'
        extra_hosts:
            - 'host.docker.internal:host-gateway'
        ports:
            - '${APP_PORT:-80}:80'
            - '${VITE_PORT:-5173}:${VITE_PORT:-5173}'
        environment:
            WWWUSER: '${WWWUSER}'
            LARAVEL_SAIL: 1
            XDEBUG_MODE: '${SAIL_XDEBUG_MODE:-off}'
            XDEBUG_CONFIG: '${SAIL_XDEBUG_CONFIG:-client_host=host.docker.internal}'
            IGNITION_LOCAL_SITES_PATH: '${PWD}'
        volumes:
            - '.:/var/www/html'
        networks:
            - sail
        depends_on:
            - pgsql
    pgsql:
        image: 'postgres:18-alpine'
        ports:
            # Bind PostgreSQL to localhost so it is not exposed to the host network.
            - '127.0.0.1:${FORWARD_DB_PORT:-5432}:5432'
        environment:
            PGPASSWORD: '${DB_PASSWORD:-secret}'
            POSTGRES_DB: '${DB_DATABASE}'
            POSTGRES_USER: '${DB_USERNAME}'
            POSTGRES_PASSWORD: '${DB_PASSWORD:-secret}'
        volumes:
            # Keep the local database across container restarts; remove this volume only to reset local data.
            - 'sail-pgsql:/var/lib/postgresql'
            - './vendor/laravel/sail/database/pgsql/create-testing-database.sql:/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/10-create-testing-database.sql'
        networks:
            - sail
        healthcheck:
            test:
                - CMD
                - pg_isready
                - '-q'
                - '-d'
                - '${DB_DATABASE}'
                - '-U'
                - '${DB_USERNAME}'
            retries: 3
            timeout: 5s
networks:
    sail:
        driver: bridge
volumes:
    sail-pgsql:
        driver: local

<?php

namespace App\Actions\Fortify;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Validator;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rule;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Laravel\Fortify\Contracts\CreatesNewUsers;

class CreateNewUser implements CreatesNewUsers
{
    use PasswordValidationRules;

    /**
     * Validate and create a newly registered user.
     *
     * @param  array<string, string>  $input
     *
     * @throws ValidationException
     */
    public function create(array $input): User
    {
        Validator::make($input, [
            'name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'email' => [
                'required',
                'string',
                'email',
                'max:255',
                Rule::unique(User::class),
            ],
            'password' => $this->passwordRules(),
        ])->validate();

        return User::create([
            'name' => $input['name'],
            'email' => $input['email'],
            'password' => Hash::make($input['password']),
        ]);
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Actions\Fortify;

use Illuminate\Contracts\Validation\Rule;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rules\Password;

trait PasswordValidationRules
{
    /**
     * Get the validation rules used to validate passwords.
     *
     * @return array<int, Rule|array<mixed>|string>
     */
    protected function passwordRules(): array
    {
        return ['required', 'string', Password::default(), 'confirmed'];
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Actions\Fortify;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Validator;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Laravel\Fortify\Contracts\ResetsUserPasswords;

class ResetUserPassword implements ResetsUserPasswords
{
    use PasswordValidationRules;

    /**
     * Validate and reset the user's forgotten password.
     *
     * @param  array<string, string>  $input
     *
     * @throws ValidationException
     */
    public function reset(User $user, array $input): void
    {
        Validator::make($input, [
            'password' => $this->passwordRules(),
        ])->validate();

        $user->forceFill([
            'password' => Hash::make($input['password']),
        ])->save();
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Actions\Fortify;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Validator;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Laravel\Fortify\Contracts\UpdatesUserPasswords;

class UpdateUserPassword implements UpdatesUserPasswords
{
    use PasswordValidationRules;

    /**
     * Validate and update the user's password.
     *
     * @param  array<string, string>  $input
     *
     * @throws ValidationException
     */
    public function update(User $user, array $input): void
    {
        Validator::make($input, [
            'current_password' => ['required', 'string', 'current_password:web'],
            'password' => $this->passwordRules(),
        ], [
            'current_password.current_password' => __('The provided password does not match your current password.'),
        ])->validateWithBag('updatePassword');

        $user->forceFill([
            'password' => Hash::make($input['password']),
        ])->save();
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Actions\Fortify;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Contracts\Auth\MustVerifyEmail;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Validator;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rule;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Laravel\Fortify\Contracts\UpdatesUserProfileInformation;

class UpdateUserProfileInformation implements UpdatesUserProfileInformation
{
    /**
     * Validate and update the given user's profile information.
     *
     * @param  array<string, string>  $input
     *
     * @throws ValidationException
     */
    public function update(User $user, array $input): void
    {
        Validator::make($input, [
            'name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],

            'email' => [
                'required',
                'string',
                'email',
                'max:255',
                Rule::unique('users')->ignore($user->id),
            ],
        ])->validateWithBag('updateProfileInformation');

        if ($input['email'] !== $user->email &&
            $user instanceof MustVerifyEmail) {
            $this->updateVerifiedUser($user, $input);
        } else {
            $user->forceFill([
                'name' => $input['name'],
                'email' => $input['email'],
            ])->save();
        }
    }

    /**
     * Update the given verified user's profile information.
     *
     * @param  array<string, string>  $input
     */
    protected function updateVerifiedUser(User $user, array $input): void
    {
        $user->forceFill([
            'name' => $input['name'],
            'email' => $input['email'],
            'email_verified_at' => null,
        ])->save();

        $user->sendEmailVerificationNotification();
    }
}


==============================================================================
FILE: app/Http/Controllers/AppointmentController.php
SIZE: 14134 bytes
==============================================================================
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Appointment;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\View\View;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rule;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Validator;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;

class AppointmentController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Show the appointment list using the signed-in user's role to limit its records.
     * Students see only their assigned appointments, teachers see appointments for
     * their assigned students, and administrators can browse the full list.
     */
    public function index(Request $request): View
    {
        $user = auth()->user();
        $filters = $request->validate([
            'search' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:100'],
            'status' => ['nullable', Rule::in(Appointment::STATUSES)],
        ]);

        $query = Appointment::with(['student', 'assignedPersonnel', 'createdBy'])->latest();

        // Scope the query before pagination so another role's appointments are never sent to the view.
        if ($user->role === 'student') {
            $query->where('student_id', $user->id);
        } elseif ($user->role === 'teacher') {
            $query->whereHas('student', fn ($students) => $students->where('assigned_teacher_id', $user->id));
        }

        if ($user->role === 'student') {
            // Hide unassigned student requests; staff can still see and schedule these requests.
            $query->whereNotNull('scheduled_at');
        } else {
            if ($status = $filters['status'] ?? null) {
                $query->where('status', $status);
            }

            $search = trim((string) ($filters['search'] ?? ''));
        }

        // Search is for staff only. Students cannot search for other students or their requests.
        if ($user->role !== 'student' && $search !== '') {
            $query->where(function ($builder) use ($search, $user) {
                $builder->where('title', 'like', "%{$search}%");
                if ($user->role !== 'student') {
                    $builder->orWhereHas('student', fn ($students) => $students->where('name', 'like', "%{$search}%"));
                }
            });
        }

        // Keep search and status filters when the user moves between result pages.
        $appointments = $query->paginate(15)->withQueryString();

        $students = in_array($user->role, ['admin', 'teacher'], true)
            ? \App\Models\User::where('role', 'student')
                ->when($user->role === 'teacher', fn ($query) => $query->where('assigned_teacher_id', $user->id))
                ->orderBy('name')->get(['id', 'name', 'email'])
            : collect();
        return view('appointments.index', compact('appointments', 'students'));
    }

    public function availableTimes(Request $request): JsonResponse|RedirectResponse
    {
        // Only staff schedule consultations; students do not receive the list of open slots.
        abort_unless(in_array($request->user()->role, ['admin', 'teacher'], true), 403);

        // The appointment form calls this endpoint with JavaScript and expects JSON, not a page.
        if (! $request->expectsJson()) {
            return redirect()->route('appointments.index');
        }

        $validated = $request->validate([
            'date' => ['required', 'date', 'after_or_equal:today', 'before_or_equal:' . now()->addDays(30)->toDateString()],
            'appointment_id' => ['nullable', 'integer', Rule::exists('appointments', 'id')],
        ]);

        // If an appointment ID is supplied, exclude that booking while assembling open slots.
        $except = isset($validated['appointment_id']) ? Appointment::findOrFail($validated['appointment_id']) : null;
        $times = collect($this->availableSlots($except))
            ->where('date', $validated['date'])
            ->map(fn ($slot) => ['value' => $slot['time'], 'label' => $slot['time_label']])
            ->values();

        return response()->json(['times' => $times]);
    }

    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $user = $request->user();

        // Students submit requests through the student-facing workflow; staff may create one for them.
        abort_unless(
            in_array($user->role, ['admin', 'teacher'], true),
            403,
            'Only teachers or administrators can create consultation requests.'
        );

        // A teacher can create requests only for students assigned to that teacher.
        // An administrator may create a request for any student account.
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'student_id' => ['required', 'integer', Rule::exists('users', 'id')->where(function ($query) use ($user) {
                $query->where('role', 'student');
                if ($user->role === 'teacher') {
                    $query->where('assigned_teacher_id', $user->id);
                }
            })],
            'title' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'reason' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:2000'],
        ]);
        // Teachers must schedule the request immediately; admins may leave it unassigned for later.
        $scheduledAt = $this->selectedSchedule($request, $user->role === 'teacher');

        // Every new request starts pending and uses the application's standard 30-minute duration.
        Appointment::create([
            'student_id' => $validated['student_id'],
            'created_by' => $user->id,
            'title' => $validated['title'],
            'reason' => $validated['reason'] ?? null,
            'scheduled_at' => $scheduledAt,
            'duration_minutes' => 30,
            'status' => 'pending',
        ]);

        return redirect()
            ->route('appointments.index')
            ->with('success', 'Consultation request submitted successfully.');
    }

    public function edit(Appointment $appointment): View
    {
        // Check ownership/assignment before loading the edit page, then restrict editing to staff.
        $this->authorizeAppointment($appointment);
        $isScheduler = in_array(auth()->user()->role, ['admin', 'teacher'], true);
        abort_unless($isScheduler, 403);

        $personnel = auth()->user()->role === 'admin'
            ? \App\Models\User::where('role', 'admin')->orderBy('name')->get(['id', 'name'])
            : collect();
        return view('appointments.edit', compact('appointment', 'personnel'));
    }

    public function update(Request $request, Appointment $appointment): RedirectResponse
    {
        // Role-specific updates are checked on the server even if the page hides certain form fields.
        $this->authorizeAppointment($appointment);
        $user = $request->user();

        // Admins can manage the workflow status and private notes. A request can only be approved
        // after it has a valid open time slot.
        if ($user->role === 'admin') {
            $validated = $request->validate([
                'status' => ['required', Rule::in(Appointment::STATUSES)],
                'assigned_to' => ['nullable', 'integer', Rule::exists('users', 'id')->where('role', 'admin')],
                'notes' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:2000'],
            ]);
            $scheduledAt = $this->selectedSchedule($request, false, $appointment);

            if ($validated['status'] === 'approved' && empty($scheduledAt)) {
                return back()->withErrors(['scheduled_date' => 'Assign an available consultation time before approving this request.'])->withInput();
            }

            $appointment->update([...$validated, 'scheduled_at' => $scheduledAt]);
        } elseif ($user->role === 'teacher') {
            // Teachers only assign times to pending requests; they cannot approve or complete them.
            abort_unless($appointment->status === 'pending', 403, 'Teachers can assign times to pending requests only.');
            $scheduledAt = $this->selectedSchedule($request, false, $appointment);
            $appointment->update(['scheduled_at' => $scheduledAt]);
        } else {
            // Students may view assigned schedule details, but cannot edit them.
            abort(403, 'Students can only view consultation dates and times assigned to them.');
        }

        return redirect()->route('appointments.index')->with('success', 'Appointment updated successfully.');
    }

    public function cancel(Appointment $appointment): RedirectResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeAppointment($appointment);
        // The cancel action changes workflow status, so it is reserved for administrators.
        abort_unless(auth()->user()->role === 'admin', 403, 'Only an administrator can change the request status.');

        $appointment->update(['status' => 'cancelled']);

        return redirect()->route('appointments.index')->with('success', 'Appointment cancelled.');
    }

    private function authorizeAppointment(Appointment $appointment): void
    {
        // Admins can access every appointment. Teachers must be assigned to the student on it,
        // and students must own it; this protects direct URLs as well as links in the interface.
        $user = auth()->user();
        $isAssignedTeacher = $user->role === 'teacher'
            && $appointment->student()->where('assigned_teacher_id', $user->id)->exists();
        $isStudentOwner = $user->role === 'student' && (int) $appointment->student_id === (int) $user->id;

        abort_unless($user->role === 'admin' || $isAssignedTeacher || $isStudentOwner, 403);
    }

    /**
     * Build future half-hour slots on weekdays during business hours for the next 30 days.
     * Pending and approved appointments reserve a slot; the appointment being edited is excluded.
     */
    private function availableSlots(?Appointment $except = null): array
    {
        $start = now()->startOfDay();
        $end = now()->addDays(30)->endOfDay();
        // Fetch existing bookings once, then compare candidate slots in memory.
        $booked = Appointment::query()
            ->whereIn('status', ['pending', 'approved'])
            ->whereNotNull('scheduled_at')
            ->when($except, fn ($query) => $query->where('id', '!=', $except->getKey()))
            ->whereBetween('scheduled_at', [$start, $end])
            ->pluck('scheduled_at')
            ->map(fn ($scheduledAt) => \Illuminate\Support\Carbon::parse($scheduledAt)->format('Y-m-d H:i'))
            ->all();

        $slots = [];
        // Start at 8:00 and step by 30 minutes, stopping before 17:00 (last slot is 16:30).
        for ($day = $start->copy(); $day <= $end; $day->addDay()) {
            if ($day->isWeekend()) {
                continue;
            }
            for ($minutes = 8 * 60; $minutes < 17 * 60; $minutes += 30) {
                $slot = $day->copy()->setTime(intdiv($minutes, 60), $minutes % 60);
                if ($slot->lte(now())) {
                    continue;
                }
                if (in_array($slot->format('Y-m-d H:i'), $booked, true)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $slots[] = [
                    'date' => $slot->format('Y-m-d'),
                    'date_label' => $slot->format('D, M j'),
                    'time' => $slot->format('H:i'),
                    'time_label' => $slot->format('g:i A'),
                ];
            }
        }

        return $slots;
    }

    private function isAvailableSlot(string $value, ?Appointment $appointment = null): bool
    {
        // Validate submitted dates independently of the date-picker list, since requests can be forged.
        $slot = \Illuminate\Support\Carbon::parse($value);
        if ($slot->lte(now()) || $slot->minute % 30 !== 0 || $slot->second !== 0 || $slot->isWeekend() || $slot->hour < 8 || $slot->hour >= 17) {
            return false;
        }

        return ! Appointment::query()
            ->when($appointment, fn ($query) => $query->where('id', '!=', $appointment->getKey()))
            ->whereIn('status', ['pending', 'approved'])
            ->whereNotNull('scheduled_at')
            ->whereDate('scheduled_at', $slot->toDateString())
            ->pluck('scheduled_at')
            ->contains(fn ($busy) => \Illuminate\Support\Carbon::parse($busy)->format('Y-m-d H:i') === $slot->format('Y-m-d H:i'));
    }

    private function selectedSchedule(Request $request, bool $required, ?Appointment $except = null): ?string
    {
        // The form posts date and time separately. Require both together, and allow both to be
        // blank only when the current role is allowed to leave the request unscheduled.
        $fields = $request->validate([
            'scheduled_date' => [Rule::requiredIf($required), 'nullable', 'date', 'after_or_equal:today'],
            'scheduled_time' => [Rule::requiredIf($required), 'nullable', 'date_format:H:i'],
        ]);

        $date = $fields['scheduled_date'] ?? null;
        $time = $fields['scheduled_time'] ?? null;
        if (! $date && ! $time) {
            return null;
        }
        if (! $date || ! $time) {
            // A half-filled schedule would leave an unusable date or time in the appointment.
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['scheduled_date' => 'Choose both a date and a time, or leave both unassigned.']);
        }

        $datetime = "$date $time";
        // Keep the current appointment's existing slot when it is submitted unchanged.
        if ($except?->scheduled_at?->format('Y-m-d H:i') === $datetime) {
            return $datetime;
        }

        // Ensure the combined datetime is valid and still in the future before checking conflicts.
        Validator::make(['scheduled_at' => $datetime], [
            'scheduled_at' => ['required', 'date', 'after:now'],
        ])->validate();

        if (! $this->isAvailableSlot($datetime, $except)) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['scheduled_time' => 'That time is unavailable. Choose another open slot.']);
        }

        return $datetime;
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Appointment;
use App\Models\CaseRecord;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rule;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class CaseRecordController extends Controller
{
    private function ensureAdmin(): void
    {
        // Case records contain private guidance information. Enforce this in the controller,
        // even when a non-admin manually enters a case URL instead of using the hidden menu link.
        abort_unless(auth()->user()?->role === 'admin', 403);
    }

    public function index(Request $request): View
    {
        $this->ensureAdmin();
        // Accept only supported filters, then apply them to the query before pagination.
        $filters = $request->validate([
            'search' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:100'],
            'status' => ['nullable', 'in:open,in_progress,closed'],
            'category' => ['nullable', 'in:academic,personal,social,career,other'],
        ]);

        // Eager-load the names and appointment displayed in each row to avoid one query per case.
        $query = CaseRecord::with(['student', 'creator', 'appointment'])->latest();
        if ($status = $filters['status'] ?? null) {
            $query->where('status', $status);
        }
        if ($category = $filters['category'] ?? null) {
            $query->where('category', $category);
        }
        if ($search = trim((string) ($filters['search'] ?? ''))) {
            $query->where(function ($builder) use ($search) {
                $builder->where('case_number', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                    ->orWhere('title', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                    ->orWhereHas('student', fn ($students) => $students->where('name', 'like', "%{$search}%"));
            });
        }
        $cases = $query->paginate(15)->withQueryString();

        return view('cases.index', compact('cases'));
    }

    public function create(): View
    {
        $this->ensureAdmin();

        // Populate the form with valid student choices and only active appointments that may be linked.
        $students = User::where('role', 'student')
            ->orderBy('name')
            ->get(['id', 'name', 'email']);

        $appointments = Appointment::with('student')
            ->whereIn('status', ['pending', 'approved'])
            ->latest()
            ->get();

        return view('cases.create', compact('students', 'appointments'));
    }

    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $this->ensureAdmin();

        // Validate ordinary fields first; the appointment rule also checks that the selected
        // appointment belongs to the selected student and is still pending or approved.
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'student_id' => ['required', 'integer', 'exists:users,id'],
            'appointment_id' => [
                'nullable',
                'integer',
                // A linked appointment must belong to this student and still be active.
                Rule::exists('appointments', 'id')->where(function ($query) use ($request) {
                    $query->where('student_id', $request->input('student_id'))
                        ->whereIn('status', ['pending', 'approved']);
                }),
            ],
            'title' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'category' => ['required', 'in:academic,personal,social,career,other'],
            'description' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:5000'],
            'remarks' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:5000'],
            'session_date' => ['nullable', 'date'],
            'session_time' => ['nullable', 'date_format:H:i'],
            'session_end_time' => ['nullable', 'date_format:H:i'],
            'findings' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:5000'],
            'recommendations' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:5000'],
            'status' => ['required', 'in:open,in_progress,closed'],
        ]);

        // `exists:users,id` alone would accept a teacher or administrator ID, so check the role too.
        abort_unless(
            User::whereKey($validated['student_id'])
                ->where('role', 'student')
                ->exists(),
            422,
            'Select a valid student account.'
        );

        // Keep a human-readable date in the reference and add a random suffix to reduce collision risk.
        $case = CaseRecord::create([
            'student_id' => $validated['student_id'],
            'created_by' => $request->user()->id,
            'appointment_id' => $validated['appointment_id'] ?? null,
            'case_number' => 'CASE-' . now()->format('Ymd') . '-' . Str::upper(Str::random(6)),
            'title' => $validated['title'],
            'category' => $validated['category'],
            'description' => $validated['description'] ?? null,
            'remarks' => $validated['remarks'] ?? null,
            'session_date' => $validated['session_date'] ?? null,
            'session_time' => $validated['session_time'] ?? null,
            'session_end_time' => $validated['session_end_time'] ?? null,
            'findings' => $validated['findings'] ?? null,
            'recommendations' => $validated['recommendations'] ?? null,
            'status' => $validated['status'],
        ]);

        return redirect()
            ->route('cases.show', $case)
            ->with('success', 'Case record created successfully.');
    }

    public function show(CaseRecord $case): View
    {
        $this->ensureAdmin();

        // Load all relationships used by the case page, including the author of each follow-up,
        // before rendering the view.
        $case->load([
            'student',
            'creator',
            'appointment',
            'followUps.creator',
        ]);

        return view('cases.show', compact('case'));
    }

    public function edit(CaseRecord $case): View
    {
        $this->ensureAdmin();
        $case->load('student');

        return view('cases.edit', compact('case'));
    }

    public function update(Request $request, CaseRecord $case): RedirectResponse
    {
        $this->ensureAdmin();
        // Keep edits within the allowed status, category, and field lengths before saving the record.
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'title' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'category' => ['required', 'in:academic,personal,social,career,other'],
            'description' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:5000'],
            'remarks' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:5000'],
            'session_date' => ['nullable', 'date'],
            'session_time' => ['nullable', 'date_format:H:i'],
            'session_end_time' => ['nullable', 'date_format:H:i'],
            'findings' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:5000'],
            'recommendations' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:5000'],
            'status' => ['required', 'in:open,in_progress,closed'],
        ]);
        $case->update($validated);

        return redirect()->route('cases.show', $case)->with('success', 'Case record updated successfully.');
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Appointment;
use App\Models\CaseRecord;
use App\Models\FollowUp;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class DashboardController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Build dashboard totals from the same role-scoped appointment query used for each person.
     * Case and follow-up totals are added only for administrators because those records are private.
     */
    public function index(): View
    {
        $user = auth()->user();

        // Start with only the appointments this user is allowed to count.
        if ($user->role === 'admin') {
            $appointments = Appointment::query();
        } elseif ($user->role === 'teacher') {
            $appointments = Appointment::query()
                ->whereHas('student', fn ($students) => $students->where('assigned_teacher_id', $user->id));
        } else {
            // Students only get counts for requests that have an assigned time.
            $appointments = Appointment::query()
                ->where('student_id', $user->id)
                ->whereNotNull('scheduled_at');
        }

        // Clone the base query so each count adds its own status filter without affecting the next one.
        $stats = [
            'pending' => (clone $appointments)->where('status', 'pending')->count(),
            'approved' => (clone $appointments)->where('status', 'approved')->count(),
            'completed' => (clone $appointments)->where('status', 'completed')->count(),
            'cancelled' => (clone $appointments)->where('status', 'cancelled')->count(),
        ];

        // Guidance-only case and follow-up counts stay off teacher and student dashboards.
        if ($user->role === 'admin') {
            $stats['open_cases'] = CaseRecord::whereIn('status', ['open', 'in_progress'])->count();
            $stats['follow_ups_today'] = FollowUp::whereDate('follow_up_date', today())->count();
        }

        return view('dashboard.index', compact('stats', 'user'));
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\CaseRecord;
use App\Models\FollowUp;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class FollowUpController extends Controller
{
    private function ensureAdmin(): void
    {
        // Follow-up notes are part of a private case history, so only guidance administrators may read or change them.
        abort_unless(auth()->user()?->role === 'admin', 403);
    }

    public function store(Request $request, CaseRecord $case): RedirectResponse
    {
        $this->ensureAdmin();

        // Validate note length and date before associating the entry with this case.
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'follow_up_date' => ['required', 'date'],
            'notes' => ['required', 'string', 'max:5000'],
            'action_taken' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:255'],
        ]);

        // Create through the parent relationship so the route's case ID is saved as case_record_id.
        // Record the currently signed-in admin as the author for the case history.
        $case->followUps()->create([
            ...$validated,
            'created_by' => $request->user()->id,
        ]);

        return redirect()->route('cases.show', $case)->with('success', 'Follow-up recorded.');
    }

    public function update(Request $request, CaseRecord $case, FollowUp $followUp): RedirectResponse
    {
        $this->ensureAdmin();
        // A follow-up ID is independently route-bound; reject it unless it belongs to this case URL.
        abort_unless((int) $followUp->case_record_id === (int) $case->id, 404);

        // Keep the original author while allowing staff to correct the date, action, or note.
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'follow_up_date' => ['required', 'date'],
            'notes' => ['required', 'string', 'max:5000'],
            'action_taken' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:255'],
        ]);

        $followUp->update($validated);

        return redirect()->route('cases.show', $case)->with('success', 'Follow-up updated.');
    }

}

FILE: app/Http/Controllers/UserController.php

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\User;
use App\Models\CaseRecord;
use App\Models\FollowUp;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rule;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class UserController extends Controller
{
    private function ensureAdmin(): void
    {
        // Keep account administration server-side: hiding these links is not an access check.
        abort_unless(auth()->user()?->role === 'admin', 403);
    }

    private function ensureTeacherOrAdmin(): void
    {
        // This role gate admits teachers and admins; each profile action separately scopes the target student.
        abort_unless(in_array(auth()->user()?->role, ['teacher', 'admin'], true), 403);
    }

    public function index(Request $request): View
    {
        $this->ensureAdmin();

        $users = User::query()
            ->select('id', 'name', 'email', 'role', 'created_at')
            ->when($request->filled('search'), function ($query) use ($request) {
                $search = $request->string('search')->trim()->toString();
                $query->where(function ($query) use ($search) {
                    $query->where('name', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                        ->orWhere('email', 'like', "%{$search}%");
                });
            })
            ->when($request->filled('role'), fn ($query) => $query->where('role', $request->input('role')))
            ->orderBy('role')->orderBy('name')->paginate(15)->withQueryString();

        return view('users.index', compact('users'));
    }

    public function students(Request $request): View
    {
        $this->ensureTeacherOrAdmin();

        // Filter by assignment in the database so teachers never receive other teachers' students.
        $students = User::query()
            ->where('role', 'student')
            ->when(auth()->user()->role === 'teacher', fn ($query) => $query->where('assigned_teacher_id', auth()->id()))
            ->when($request->filled('search'), function ($query) use ($request) {
                $search = $request->string('search')->trim()->toString();
                $query->where(fn ($query) => $query->where('name', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                    ->orWhere('student_number', 'like', "%{$search}%"));
            })
            ->orderBy('name')
            ->paginate(15)
            ->withQueryString();

        return view('users.students', compact('students'));
    }

    public function editStudentDetails(User $user): View
    {
        $this->ensureTeacherOrAdmin();
        // The route accepts any user ID, so reject non-students and unassigned teacher targets.
        abort_unless($user->role === 'student', 404);
        $this->ensureStudentIsAssignedToCurrentTeacher($user);

        return view('users.student-details', compact('user'));
    }

    public function updateStudentDetails(Request $request, User $user): RedirectResponse
    {
        $this->ensureTeacherOrAdmin();
        // Repeat the checks on writes; a user cannot bypass them by submitting a direct form request.
        abort_unless($user->role === 'student', 404);
        $this->ensureStudentIsAssignedToCurrentTeacher($user);

        $validated = $request->validate([
            'grade_level' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:50'],
            'section' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:100'],
            'contact_number' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:50'],
            'address' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:1000'],
            'guardian_name' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'guardian_relationship' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:100'],
            'guardian_contact_number' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:50'],
        ]);

        $user->update($validated);

        return redirect()->route('students.index')->with('success', 'Student details updated successfully.');
    }

    public function create(): View
    {
        $this->ensureAdmin();

        $teachers = User::where('role', 'teacher')->orderBy('name')->get(['id', 'name']);

        return view('users.create', compact('teachers'));
    }

    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $this->ensureAdmin();

        // Role and teacher IDs are checked against allowed values/roles before an account is created.
        // Student-only fields are required only when the selected account is a student.
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'email' => ['required', 'email', 'max:255', 'unique:users,email'],
            'role' => ['required', Rule::in(['admin', 'teacher', 'student'])],
            'assigned_teacher_id' => ['nullable', 'integer', Rule::exists('users', 'id')->where(fn ($query) => $query->where('role', 'teacher'))],
            'student_number' => ['required_if:role,student', 'nullable', 'string', 'max:50', Rule::unique('users', 'student_number')],
            'grade_level' => ['required_if:role,student', 'nullable', 'string', 'max:50'],
            'section' => ['required_if:role,student', 'nullable', 'string', 'max:100'],
            'contact_number' => ['required_if:role,student', 'nullable', 'string', 'max:50'],
            'address' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:1000'],
            'guardian_name' => ['required_if:role,student', 'nullable', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'guardian_relationship' => ['required_if:role,student', 'nullable', 'string', 'max:100'],
            'guardian_contact_number' => ['required_if:role,student', 'nullable', 'string', 'max:50'],
            'password' => ['required', 'string', 'min:8', 'confirmed'],
        ]);

        // Teacher and admin accounts do not use the student and guardian fields.
        if ($validated['role'] !== 'student') {
            $validated = array_merge($validated, $this->emptyStudentProfile());
        }

        User::create($validated);

        return redirect()->route('users.index')
            ->with('success', 'Account created successfully.');
    }

    public function edit(User $user): View
    {
        $this->ensureAdmin();

        $teachers = User::where('role', 'teacher')->orderBy('name')->get(['id', 'name']);

        return view('users.edit', compact('user', 'teachers'));
    }

    public function update(Request $request, User $user): RedirectResponse
    {
        $this->ensureAdmin();

        $validated = $request->validate([
            'name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'email' => [
                'required', 'email', 'max:255',
                Rule::unique('users', 'email')->ignore($user->id),
            ],
            'role' => ['required', Rule::in(['admin', 'teacher', 'student'])],
            'assigned_teacher_id' => ['nullable', 'integer', Rule::exists('users', 'id')->where(fn ($query) => $query->where('role', 'teacher'))],
            'student_number' => ['required_if:role,student', 'nullable', 'string', 'max:50', Rule::unique('users', 'student_number')->ignore($user->id)],
            'grade_level' => ['required_if:role,student', 'nullable', 'string', 'max:50'],
            'section' => ['required_if:role,student', 'nullable', 'string', 'max:100'],
            'contact_number' => ['required_if:role,student', 'nullable', 'string', 'max:50'],
            'address' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:1000'],
            'guardian_name' => ['required_if:role,student', 'nullable', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'guardian_relationship' => ['required_if:role,student', 'nullable', 'string', 'max:100'],
            'guardian_contact_number' => ['required_if:role,student', 'nullable', 'string', 'max:50'],
        ]);

        // Clear old student/guardian details when an account is changed to staff.
        if ($validated['role'] !== 'student') {
            $validated = array_merge($validated, $this->emptyStudentProfile());
        }

        // Prevent an administrator from removing their own access through account management.
        if (
            $user->id === $request->user()->id &&
            $validated['role'] !== 'admin'
        ) {
            return back()->withErrors([
                'role' => 'You cannot remove your own administrator role.',
            ])->withInput();
        }

        // Keep at least one administrator so the system cannot lose its only account manager.
        if (
            $user->role === 'admin' &&
            $validated['role'] !== 'admin' &&
            User::where('role', 'admin')->count() <= 1
        ) {
            return back()->withErrors([
                'role' => 'The system must retain at least one administrator.',
            ])->withInput();
        }

        $user->update($validated);

        return redirect()->route('users.index')
            ->with('success', 'Account updated successfully.');
    }

    public function resetPassword(Request $request, User $user): RedirectResponse
    {
        $this->ensureAdmin();

        $validated = $request->validate([
            'password' => ['required', 'string', 'min:8', 'confirmed'],
        ]);

        // Hash the new password before persistence; never store the submitted password as plain text.
        $user->password = Hash::make($validated['password']);
        $user->save();

        return redirect()->route('users.edit', $user)
            ->with('success', 'Password reset successfully.');
    }

    public function destroy(User $user): RedirectResponse
    {
        $this->ensureAdmin();

        // Preserve a way for the current administrator to sign in and manage the system.
        if ($user->id === auth()->id()) {
            return back()->withErrors([
                'delete' => 'You cannot delete your own account.',
            ]);
        }

        if (
            $user->role === 'admin' &&
            User::where('role', 'admin')->count() <= 1
        ) {
            return back()->withErrors([
                'delete' => 'The last administrator account cannot be deleted.',
            ]);
        }

        // Preserve historical appointments, cases, and notes: deleting a linked account would
        // either break those records or remove the identity that explains who created them.
        if (
            $user->appointmentsAsStudent()->exists() ||
            $user->appointmentsAssigned()->exists() ||
            $user->createdCases()->exists() ||
            CaseRecord::where('student_id', $user->id)->exists() ||
            FollowUp::where('created_by', $user->id)->exists()
        ) {
            return back()->withErrors([
                'delete' => 'This account has related records. Preserve its history instead of deleting it.',
            ]);
        }

        $user->delete();

        return redirect()->route('users.index')
            ->with('success', 'Account deleted successfully.');
    }

    private function emptyStudentProfile(): array
    {
        return [
            'assigned_teacher_id' => null,
            'student_number' => null,
            'grade_level' => null,
            'section' => null,
            'contact_number' => null,
            'address' => null,
            'guardian_name' => null,
            'guardian_relationship' => null,
            'guardian_contact_number' => null,
        ];
    }

    private function ensureStudentIsAssignedToCurrentTeacher(User $student): void
    {
        if (auth()->user()->role === 'teacher') {
            // Return 404 rather than revealing whether another teacher's student account exists.
            abort_unless((int) $student->assigned_teacher_id === (int) auth()->id(), 404);
        }
    }
}

FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Api/GuidanceApiController.php
SIZE: 13901 bytes

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Api;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\Appointment;
use App\Models\CaseRecord;
use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rule;

class GuidanceApiController extends Controller
{
    // Returns appointment data as JSON, filtered by the signed-in API user's role.
    public function appointments(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        // Validate filters before building the query so unsupported status values are rejected.
        $filters = $request->validate([
            'status' => ['nullable', Rule::in(Appointment::STATUSES)],
        ]);
        $query = Appointment::query()->latest();
        // Scope rows at the database level: students see their scheduled appointments only,
        // teachers see their assigned students' appointments, and admins see all appointments.
        if ($request->user()->role === 'student') {
            $query->where('student_id', $request->user()->id)
                ->whereNotNull('scheduled_at');
        } elseif ($request->user()->role === 'teacher') {
            $query->whereHas('student', fn ($students) => $students->where('assigned_teacher_id', $request->user()->id));
        }
        if ($request->user()->role !== 'student' && ($status = $filters['status'] ?? null)) {
            $query->where('status', $status);
        }

        // Keep the student response minimal; staff receive the broader appointment representation.
        return response()->json($query->paginate(20)->through(function (Appointment $appointment) use ($request) {
            return $request->user()->role === 'student'
                ? [
                    'title' => $appointment->title,
                    'scheduled_at' => $appointment->scheduled_at,
                    'status' => $appointment->status,
                ]
                : $this->appointmentData($appointment);
        }));
    }

    public function createAppointment(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        // Only staff can create requests. Teachers must select a student assigned to them and a time;
        // an admin may create an unassigned request for any student.
        $user = $request->user();
        abort_unless(in_array($user->role, ['admin', 'teacher'], true), 403);
        $data = $request->validate([
            'student_id' => ['required', 'integer', Rule::exists('users', 'id')->where(function ($query) use ($user) {
                $query->where('role', 'student');
                if ($user->role === 'teacher') {
                    $query->where('assigned_teacher_id', $user->id);
                }
            })],
            'title' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'reason' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:2000'],
            'scheduled_at' => [Rule::requiredIf($user->role === 'teacher'), 'nullable', 'date', 'after:now'],
        ]);
        // A valid date is not enough: enforce the same business-hour and conflict rules as the web form.
        if (! empty($data['scheduled_at']) && ! $this->isAvailableSlot($data['scheduled_at'])) {
            return response()->json(['message' => 'That time is unavailable. Choose another slot.'], 422);
        }

        $appointment = Appointment::create([
            ...$data,
            'student_id' => $data['student_id'],
            'created_by' => $user->id,
            'scheduled_at' => $data['scheduled_at'] ?? null,
            'duration_minutes' => 30,
            'status' => 'pending',
        ]);

        return response()->json(['data' => $this->appointmentData($appointment)], 201);
    }

    public function showAppointment(Request $request, Appointment $appointment): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeAppointment($request, $appointment);

        if ($request->user()->role === 'student') {
            // Do not expose an unassigned request or staff-only fields to a student.
            abort_unless($appointment->scheduled_at, 404);
            return response()->json(['data' => [
                'title' => $appointment->title,
                'scheduled_at' => $appointment->scheduled_at,
                'status' => $appointment->status,
            ]]);
        }

        return response()->json(['data' => $this->appointmentData($appointment)]);
    }

    public function updateAppointment(Request $request, Appointment $appointment): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeAppointment($request, $appointment);
        $user = $request->user();
        // Students may read limited schedule details, but only staff may change appointment records through the API.
        abort_unless($user->role !== 'student', 403, 'Students can only view assigned consultation dates, times, and status.');

        // Whitelist update fields by role so callers cannot change fields outside their responsibility.
        if ($user->role === 'admin') {
            $data = $request->validate([
                'status' => ['sometimes', 'required', Rule::in(Appointment::STATUSES)],
                'scheduled_at' => ['sometimes', 'nullable', 'date', 'after:now'],
                'assigned_to' => ['sometimes', 'nullable', 'integer', Rule::exists('users', 'id')->where('role', 'admin')],
                'notes' => ['sometimes', 'nullable', 'string', 'max:2000'],
            ]);
            abort_if($data === [], 422, 'Provide at least one field to update.');
            if (! empty($data['scheduled_at']) && ! $this->isAvailableSlot($data['scheduled_at'], $appointment)) {
                return response()->json(['message' => 'That time is unavailable. Choose another slot.'], 422);
            }
            // Approval without an assigned time would create an unusable appointment.
            if (($data['status'] ?? null) === 'approved' && empty($data['scheduled_at'] ?? $appointment->scheduled_at)) {
                return response()->json(['message' => 'Assign an available consultation time before approving.'], 422);
            }
        } elseif ($user->role === 'teacher') {
            // Teachers may assign a time only while the request is pending; status remains admin-controlled.
            abort_unless($appointment->status === 'pending', 403, 'Teachers can assign times to pending requests only.');
            $data = $request->validate([
                'scheduled_at' => ['sometimes', 'nullable', 'date', 'after:now'],
            ]);
            abort_if($data === [], 422, 'Provide an available scheduled_at time.');
            if (! empty($data['scheduled_at']) && ! $this->isAvailableSlot($data['scheduled_at'], $appointment)) {
                return response()->json(['message' => 'That time is unavailable. Choose another slot.'], 422);
            }
        } else {
            abort_unless($appointment->status === 'pending', 403);
            $data = $request->validate([
                'title' => ['sometimes', 'required', 'string', 'max:255'],
                'reason' => ['sometimes', 'nullable', 'string', 'max:2000'],
            ]);
            abort_if($data === [], 422, 'Provide at least one field to update.');
        }

        $appointment->update($data);

        return response()->json(['data' => $this->appointmentData($appointment->refresh())]);
    }

    public function cancelAppointment(Request $request, Appointment $appointment): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeAppointment($request, $appointment);
        abort_unless($request->user()->role === 'admin', 403, 'Only an administrator can change the request status.');
        $appointment->update(['status' => 'cancelled']);

        return response()->json(['data' => $this->appointmentData($appointment->refresh())]);
    }

    public function cases(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        // Case details and follow-up notes are confidential and are available to administrators only.
        $this->ensureAdmin($request);
        $filters = $request->validate([
            'status' => ['nullable', 'in:open,in_progress,closed'],
            'search' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:100'],
        ]);
        $query = CaseRecord::with('student')->latest();
        if ($status = $filters['status'] ?? null) {
            $query->where('status', $status);
        }
        if ($search = $filters['search'] ?? null) {
            $query->where(fn ($builder) => $builder->where('case_number', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                ->orWhere('title', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                ->orWhereHas('student', fn ($students) => $students->where('name', 'like', "%{$search}%")));
        }

        return response()->json($query->paginate(20)->through(fn (CaseRecord $case) => $this->caseData($case)));
    }

    public function showCase(Request $request, CaseRecord $case): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->ensureAdmin($request);
        // Load the relationships used by the detailed response in one pass.
        $case->load(['student', 'followUps.creator']);

        return response()->json(['data' => $this->caseData($case, true)]);
    }

    public function updateCaseStatus(Request $request, CaseRecord $case): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->ensureAdmin($request);
        $data = $request->validate(['status' => ['required', 'in:open,in_progress,closed']]);
        $case->update($data);
        $case->load('student');

        return response()->json(['data' => $this->caseData($case->refresh())]);
    }

    public function followUps(Request $request, CaseRecord $case): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->ensureAdmin($request);

        // The case relationship scopes entries to this case; return creator identity without extra user fields.
        return response()->json(['data' => $case->followUps()->with('creator:id,name')->get()]);
    }

    public function createFollowUp(Request $request, CaseRecord $case): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->ensureAdmin($request);
        // Validate the note, then create it through its parent case so case_record_id cannot be spoofed.
        $data = $request->validate([
            'follow_up_date' => ['required', 'date'],
            'notes' => ['required', 'string', 'max:5000'],
            'action_taken' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:255'],
        ]);
        $followUp = $case->followUps()->create([...$data, 'created_by' => $request->user()->id]);

        return response()->json(['data' => $followUp->load('creator:id,name')], 201);
    }

    private function appointmentData(Appointment $appointment): array
    {
        // This is the staff JSON format. Internal appointment notes are deliberately omitted.
        return [
            'id' => $appointment->id,
            'student_id' => $appointment->student_id,
            'title' => $appointment->title,
            'reason' => $appointment->reason,
            'scheduled_at' => $appointment->scheduled_at,
            'duration_minutes' => $appointment->duration_minutes,
            'status' => $appointment->status,
            'created_at' => $appointment->created_at,
        ];
    }

    private function isAvailableSlot(string $value, ?Appointment $appointment = null): bool
    {
        // Treat only future half-hour weekday slots between 08:00 and 17:00 as valid.
        $slot = \Illuminate\Support\Carbon::parse($value);
        if ($slot->minute % 30 !== 0 || $slot->second !== 0 || $slot->isWeekend() || $slot->hour < 8 || $slot->hour >= 17) {
            return false;
        }

        // Pending and approved bookings reserve their date/time; completed and cancelled ones do not.
        return ! Appointment::query()
            ->when($appointment, fn ($query) => $query->where('id', '!=', $appointment->getKey()))
            ->whereIn('status', ['pending', 'approved'])
            ->whereNotNull('scheduled_at')
            ->whereDate('scheduled_at', $slot->toDateString())
            ->pluck('scheduled_at')
            ->contains(fn ($busy) => \Illuminate\Support\Carbon::parse($busy)->format('Y-m-d H:i') === $slot->format('Y-m-d H:i'));
    }

    private function caseData(CaseRecord $case, bool $includeFollowUps = false): array
    {
        // Build one consistent JSON format for case lists and case details.
        $data = [
            'id' => $case->id,
            'case_number' => $case->case_number,
            'student' => $case->student?->only(['id', 'name']),
            'appointment_id' => $case->appointment_id,
            'title' => $case->title,
            'category' => $case->category,
            'description' => $case->description,
            'status' => $case->status,
            'created_at' => $case->created_at,
        ];

        if ($includeFollowUps) {
            // Include private follow-up content only in the admin-only detailed case response, not list rows.
            $data['follow_ups'] = $case->followUps->map(fn ($followUp) => [
                'id' => $followUp->id,
                'date' => $followUp->follow_up_date,
                'notes' => $followUp->notes,
                'action_taken' => $followUp->action_taken,
                'created_by' => $followUp->creator?->only(['id', 'name']),
            ]);
        }

        return $data;
    }

    private function authorizeAppointment(Request $request, Appointment $appointment): void
    {
        // Basic authentication identifies the caller; this ownership check decides which record they may access.
        $user = $request->user();
        $isAssignedTeacher = $user->role === 'teacher'
            && $appointment->student()->where('assigned_teacher_id', $user->id)->exists();
        $isStudentOwner = $user->role === 'student' && (int) $appointment->student_id === (int) $user->id;

        abort_unless($user->role === 'admin' || $isAssignedTeacher || $isStudentOwner, 403);
    }

    private function ensureAdmin(Request $request): void
    {
        abort_unless($request->user()->role === 'admin', 403);
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Middleware;

use Closure;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response;

class AppendMis215EggHeader
{
    public function handle(Request $request, Closure $next): Response
    {
        $response = $next($request);
        $response->headers->set('X-MIS215-Egg', 'Durana');

        return $response;
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;

class Appointment extends Model
{
    // Mass-assignable fields used after controllers validate a create or update request.
    protected $fillable = [
        'student_id',
        'created_by',
        'assigned_to',
        'title',
        'reason',
        'scheduled_at',
        'duration_minutes',
        'status',
        'notes',
    ];

    // Allowed appointment states; controllers validate transitions and require a time before approval.
    public const STATUSES = ['pending', 'approved', 'completed', 'cancelled'];

    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'scheduled_at' => 'datetime',
        ];
    }

    public function student(): BelongsTo
    {
        // The student who will attend this consultation.
        return $this->belongsTo(User::class, 'student_id');
    }

    public function assignedPersonnel(): BelongsTo
    {
        // Optional administrator responsible for handling this consultation.
        return $this->belongsTo(User::class, 'assigned_to');
    }

    public function createdBy(): BelongsTo
    {
        // Staff account that originally submitted the request; retained for audit context.
        return $this->belongsTo(User::class, 'created_by');
    }
}
